// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
fatal: not a git repository (or any parent up to mount point /)

codex
The main opportunity is broader than adding a pragma: each seed is independent within a QT round, and the current candidate builder also recomputes every prior member distance on every insertion. I’m preserving the deterministic “lowest seed/candidate wins ties” behavior while combining per-seed OpenMP work with reusable per-thread scratch state and incremental diameter updates.

+    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
+    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the

+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);

+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||

+        const bool first_member = workspace.members.size() == 1;
+
+        // A candidate's diameter can only change when the newest member is
+        // added.  Updating the saved maximum produces the same values, in the
+        // same order, as rescanning every prior member.
+        for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] ||
+                workspace.membership_epoch[candidate] == workspace.epoch) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[newest_member]);
+            const double max_dist = first_member
+                ? dist
+                : std::max(workspace.max_distances[candidate], dist);
+            workspace.max_distances[candidate] = max_dist;
+
+            // Strict comparisons and ascending candidate traversal preserve
+            // the sequential implementation's deterministic tie breaking.
+            if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
+                min_diameter = max_dist;
+                closest = candidate;
+            }
+        }
         
         if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
         
-        in_cluster[closest] = true;
-        members.push_back(closest);
-    }

-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;

+        workspace.members.push_back(closest);
+        newest_member = closest;
     }
     
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());

codex
The first implementation now builds cleanly with OpenMP required by CMake. I’m checking two things next: bit-for-bit cluster membership across thread counts and whether scheduling/thread-team overhead stays controlled as the active seed set shrinks.

+// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
+// contending during a clustering round.
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    std::vector<int> members;
+};

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate
+    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
+    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the
+    // same lowest-index seed on cardinality ties as the original loop.
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()));
+    size_t round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) \
+        shared(points, clustered, unclustered_indices, clusters, thread_best, \
+               round_size, finished) firstprivate(N, threshold)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+
+        while (true) {
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = finished || unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = unclustered_indices.size();
+            }
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+

+            local_best.cardinality = -1;
+            local_best.seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+            local_best.members.clear();
+
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {

+            {

+                int max_cardinality = -1;

+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
+                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
+                        winning_thread = thread;
+                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
+                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(
+                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(

-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }

/bin/bash -lc "set -e
for spec in '1 2.0' '2 100.0' '10 0.1' '10 100.0' '50 0.75' '150 2.0' '400 2.0'; do
  set -- "'$spec
  n=$1
  threshold=$2
  /tmp/qtcluster-baseline-build/qtclustering -n "$n" -t "$threshold" -r -v | sed -E '"'/"'^(Clustering time|Performance):?/d'"' > \"/tmp/ref-"'${n}-${threshold}.txt"
  OMP_NUM_THREADS=7 /tmp/qtcluster-openmp-build/qtclustering -n "$n" -t "$threshold" -r -v | sed -E '"'/"'^(Clustering time|Performance):?/d'"' > \"/tmp/omp-"'${n}-${threshold}.txt"
  diff -u "/tmp/ref-${n}-${threshold}.txt" "/tmp/omp-${n}-${threshold}.txt"
  echo "matched n=$n threshold=$threshold"
done
for threads in 1 3 16 64 128 256; do
  OMP_NUM_THREADS=$threads OMP_PROC_BIND=spread OMP_PLACES=cores /usr/bin/time -f "threads=$threads elapsed=%e" /tmp/qtcluster-openmp-build/qtclustering -n 1000 -t 2.0 -r > "/tmp/determinism-$threads.txt"
  rg '"'Clustering time|Clusters found|Hash:' \"/tmp/determinism-"'$threads.txt" | tr '"'\\n' ' '
  echo
done" in /home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r2

+// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
+// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.
+struct CandidateWorkspace {
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)

+// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
+// contending during a clustering round.
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    std::vector<int> members;

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate
+    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
+    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the
+    // same lowest-index seed on cardinality ties as the original loop.
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()));

+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) \

+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+
+        while (true) {
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = finished || unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = unclustered_indices.size();
+            }
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+

+            local_best.cardinality = -1;
+            local_best.seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+            local_best.members.clear();
+

+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {

+                int max_cardinality = -1;

+
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
+                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
+                        winning_thread = thread;
+                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
+                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(
+                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),

+// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
+// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.

+        // added.  Updating the saved maximum produces the same values, in the

+// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
+// contending during a clustering round.
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    std::vector<int> members;

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate
+    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
+    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the
+    // same lowest-index seed on cardinality ties as the original loop.
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()));
+    size_t round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) \
+        shared(points, clustered, unclustered_indices, clusters, thread_best, \
+               round_size, finished) firstprivate(N, threshold)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+
+        while (true) {
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = finished || unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = unclustered_indices.size();
+            }
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            ThreadBest& local_best = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread_id)];
+            local_best.cardinality = -1;
+            local_best.seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+            local_best.members.clear();
+
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, points, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }

+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int winning_thread = -1;
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
+                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
+                        winning_thread = thread;
+                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
+                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(
+                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&clustered](const int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),

+                }

-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()

-            break;

+inline double squaredDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;

+// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it

+        const bool first_member = workspace.members.size() == 1;

+        // added.  Updating the saved maximum produces the same values, in the
+        // same order, as rescanning every prior member.
+        for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
+            if (workspace.membership_epoch[candidate] == workspace.epoch) {

+                ? squared_dist
+                : std::max(workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate], squared_dist);
+            workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate] = max_squared;

+            // Strict comparisons and ascending candidate traversal preserve
+            // the sequential implementation's deterministic tie breaking.
+            // sqrt is monotonic, so only a new squared-distance record can

+            // removing it from nearly all inner-loop iterations.

+// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
+// contending during a clustering round.
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    std::vector<int> members;

+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);

+            #pragma omp single

+                finished = finished || unclustered_indices.empty();

+

+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
+                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
+                        winning_thread = thread;
+                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
+                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(
+                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));

+                            [&clustered](const int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),

+    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the

Membership hashes and validation now match the reference across varied thresholds and 1–256 threads. On the 1,000-point case, the optimized single-thread kernel is about 6.8× faster than the original, and OpenMP reaches roughly 31× additional speedup at 64 threads; I’m doing broader edge-threshold and sanitizer checks before finalizing.
The deterministic parallel kernel and required OpenMP linkage are implemented; reference comparisons passed, so validation is now focused on numerical edge cases, runtime safety, and scaling.

// QT Clustering Benchmark - OpenMP Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

inline double squaredDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
}

// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.
struct CandidateWorkspace {
    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
        : membership_epoch(static_cast<size_t>(point_count), 0),
          max_squared_distances(static_cast<size_t>(point_count)) {
        members.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(point_count));
    }

    void startCandidate(const int seed_point) {
        ++epoch;
        if (epoch == 0) {
            std::fill(membership_epoch.begin(), membership_epoch.end(), 0);
            ++epoch;
        }
        members.clear();
        members.push_back(seed_point);
        membership_epoch[seed_point] = epoch;
    }

    std::vector<int> members;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> membership_epoch;
    std::vector<double> max_squared_distances;
    std::uint32_t epoch = 0;
};

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {
    workspace.startCandidate(seed_point);
    int newest_member = seed_point;
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (workspace.members.size() < unclustered_indices.size()) {
        int closest = -1;
        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
        double smallest_squared = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
        const bool first_member = workspace.members.size() == 1;

        // A candidate's diameter can only change when the newest member is
        // added.  Updating the saved maximum produces the same values, in the
        // same order, as rescanning every prior member.
        for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
            if (workspace.membership_epoch[candidate] == workspace.epoch) {
                continue;
            }

            const double squared_dist = squaredDistance(
                points[candidate], points[newest_member]);
            const double max_squared = first_member
                ? squared_dist
                : std::max(workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate], squared_dist);
            workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate] = max_squared;

            // Strict comparisons and ascending candidate traversal preserve
            // the sequential implementation's deterministic tie breaking.
            // sqrt is monotonic, so only a new squared-distance record can
            // improve the winner.  Computing sqrt for those records retains
            // the original floating-point threshold and tie semantics while
            // removing it from nearly all inner-loop iterations.
            if (max_squared < smallest_squared) {
                smallest_squared = max_squared;
                const double max_dist = std::sqrt(max_squared);
                if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
                    min_diameter = max_dist;
                    closest = candidate;
                }
            }
        }
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        workspace.membership_epoch[closest] = workspace.epoch;
        workspace.members.push_back(closest);
        newest_member = closest;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());
}

// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
// contending during a clustering round.
struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
    int cardinality = -1;
    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
    std::vector<int> members;
};

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate
    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the
    // same lowest-index seed on cardinality ties as the original loop.
    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()));
    size_t round_size = 0;
    bool finished = false;

    #pragma omp parallel default(none) \
        shared(points, clustered, unclustered_indices, clusters, thread_best, \
               round_size, finished) firstprivate(N, threshold)
    {
        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);

        while (true) {
            #pragma omp single
            {
                finished = finished || unclustered_indices.empty();
                round_size = unclustered_indices.size();
            }

            if (finished) {
                break;
            }

            ThreadBest& local_best = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread_id)];
            local_best.cardinality = -1;
            local_best.seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
            local_best.members.clear();

            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
            for (size_t i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold, workspace);

                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {
                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
                    local_best.seed = seed;
                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
                }
            }

            #pragma omp single
            {
                int winning_thread = -1;
                int max_cardinality = -1;
                int best_seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();

                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
                        winning_thread = thread;
                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
                    }
                }

                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
                    Cluster cluster;
                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
                    cluster.members = std::move(
                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);

                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
                        clustered[member] = 1;
                    }
                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));

                    unclustered_indices.erase(
                        std::remove_if(
                            unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                            [&clustered](const int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
                        unclustered_indices.end());
                } else {
                    finished = true;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
    
    return valid;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

+inline double squaredDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
+    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
+    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
+}
+
+// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
+// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.
+struct CandidateWorkspace {
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
+        : membership_epoch(static_cast<size_t>(point_count), 0),
+          max_squared_distances(static_cast<size_t>(point_count)) {
+        members.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(point_count));
+    }
+
+    void startCandidate(const int seed_point) {
+        ++epoch;
+        if (epoch == 0) {
+            std::fill(membership_epoch.begin(), membership_epoch.end(), 0);
+            ++epoch;
         }

-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
-        }
+        members.clear();
+        members.push_back(seed_point);
+        membership_epoch[seed_point] = epoch;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}
 
+    std::vector<int> members;
+    std::vector<std::uint32_t> membership_epoch;
+    std::vector<double> max_squared_distances;
+    std::uint32_t epoch = 0;
+};

+    while (workspace.members.size() < unclustered_indices.size()) {
+        int closest = -1;
+        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+        double smallest_squared = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+        const bool first_member = workspace.members.size() == 1;
+
+        // A candidate's diameter can only change when the newest member is
+        // added.  Updating the saved maximum produces the same values, in the
+        // same order, as rescanning every prior member.
+        for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
+            if (workspace.membership_epoch[candidate] == workspace.epoch) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            const double squared_dist = squaredDistance(
+                points[candidate], points[newest_member]);
+            const double max_squared = first_member
+                ? squared_dist
+                : std::max(workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate], squared_dist);
+            workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate] = max_squared;
+
+            // Strict comparisons and ascending candidate traversal preserve
+            // the sequential implementation's deterministic tie breaking.
+            // sqrt is monotonic, so only a new squared-distance record can
+            // improve the winner.  Computing sqrt for those records retains
+            // the original floating-point threshold and tie semantics while
+            // removing it from nearly all inner-loop iterations.
+            if (max_squared < smallest_squared) {
+                smallest_squared = max_squared;
+                const double max_dist = std::sqrt(max_squared);
+                if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
+                    min_diameter = max_dist;
+                    closest = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }

+        workspace.membership_epoch[closest] = workspace.epoch;

-    }

-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());

+// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
+// contending during a clustering round.
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    std::vector<int> members;
+};

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate

+inline double squaredDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
+    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
+    return dx * dx + dy * dy;

+// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
+// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.
+struct CandidateWorkspace {
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
+        : membership_epoch(static_cast<size_t>(point_count), 0),
+          max_squared_distances(static_cast<size_t>(point_count)) {
+        members.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(point_count));
+    }
+
+    void startCandidate(const int seed_point) {
+        ++epoch;
+        if (epoch == 0) {
+            std::fill(membership_epoch.begin(), membership_epoch.end(), 0);
+            ++epoch;

-        }

+        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();

+        const bool first_member = workspace.members.size() == 1;

+        // A candidate's diameter can only change when the newest member is
+        // added.  Updating the saved maximum produces the same values, in the
+        // same order, as rescanning every prior member.
+        for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
+            if (workspace.membership_epoch[candidate] == workspace.epoch) {

+            workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate] = max_squared;
+
+            // Strict comparisons and ascending candidate traversal preserve
+            // the sequential implementation's deterministic tie breaking.
+            // sqrt is monotonic, so only a new squared-distance record can
+            // improve the winner.  Computing sqrt for those records retains
+            // the original floating-point threshold and tie semantics while
+            // removing it from nearly all inner-loop iterations.

+// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
+// contending during a clustering round.
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    std::vector<int> members;

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate
+    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
+    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the
+    // same lowest-index seed on cardinality ties as the original loop.
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()));

+                round_size = unclustered_indices.size();

+                break;

+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||

+                int max_cardinality = -1;

+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
+                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
+                        winning_thread = thread;
+                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
+                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(
+                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&clustered](const int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());

+                    finished = true;
+                }

-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }

    75	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    76	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    77	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    78	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    79	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    80	}
    81	
    82	inline double squaredDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    83	    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    84	    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    85	    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
    86	}
    87	
    88	// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
    89	// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.
    90	struct CandidateWorkspace {
    91	    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
    92	        : membership_epoch(static_cast<size_t>(point_count), 0),
    93	          max_squared_distances(static_cast<size_t>(point_count)) {
    94	        members.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(point_count));
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    void startCandidate(const int seed_point) {
    98	        ++epoch;
    99	        if (epoch == 0) {
   100	            std::fill(membership_epoch.begin(), membership_epoch.end(), 0);
   101	            ++epoch;
   102	        }
   103	        members.clear();
   104	        members.push_back(seed_point);
   105	        membership_epoch[seed_point] = epoch;
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    std::vector<int> members;
   109	    std::vector<std::uint32_t> membership_epoch;
   110	    std::vector<double> max_squared_distances;
   111	    std::uint32_t epoch = 0;
   112	};
   113	
   114	// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
   115	// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
   116	int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
   117	                              const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
   118	                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
   119	                              const double threshold,
   120	                              CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {
   121	    workspace.startCandidate(seed_point);
   122	    int newest_member = seed_point;
   123	    
   124	    // Iteratively add closest points
   125	    while (workspace.members.size() < unclustered_indices.size()) {
   126	        int closest = -1;
   127	        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
   128	        double smallest_squared = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
   129	        const bool first_member = workspace.members.size() == 1;
   130	
   131	        // A candidate's diameter can only change when the newest member is
   132	        // added.  Updating the saved maximum produces the same values, in the
   133	        // same order, as rescanning every prior member.
   134	        for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
   135	            if (workspace.membership_epoch[candidate] == workspace.epoch) {
   136	                continue;
   137	            }
   138	
   139	            const double squared_dist = squaredDistance(
   140	                points[candidate], points[newest_member]);
   141	            const double max_squared = first_member
   142	                ? squared_dist
   143	                : std::max(workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate], squared_dist);
   144	            workspace.max_squared_distances[candidate] = max_squared;
   145	
   146	            // Strict comparisons and ascending candidate traversal preserve
   147	            // the sequential implementation's deterministic tie breaking.
   148	            // sqrt is monotonic, so only a new squared-distance record can
   149	            // improve the winner.  Computing sqrt for those records retains
   150	            // the original floating-point threshold and tie semantics while
   151	            // removing it from nearly all inner-loop iterations.
   152	            if (max_squared < smallest_squared) {
   153	                smallest_squared = max_squared;
   154	                const double max_dist = std::sqrt(max_squared);
   155	                if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
   156	                    min_diameter = max_dist;
   157	                    closest = candidate;
   158	                }
   159	            }
   160	        }
   161	        
   162	        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
   163	        
   164	        workspace.membership_epoch[closest] = workspace.epoch;
   165	        workspace.members.push_back(closest);
   166	        newest_member = closest;
   167	    }
   168	    
   169	    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());
   170	}
   171	
   172	// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
   173	// contending during a clustering round.
   174	struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
   175	    int cardinality = -1;
   176	    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
   177	    std::vector<int> members;
   178	};
   179	
   180	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   181	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   182	                                  const double threshold) {
   183	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   184	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(static_cast<size_t>(N), 0);
   185	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
   186	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   187	    
   188	    // Initialize unclustered indices
   189	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   190	        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
   191	    }
   192	    
   193	    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate
   194	    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
   195	    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the
   196	    // same lowest-index seed on cardinality ties as the original loop.
   197	    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()));
   198	    size_t round_size = 0;
   199	    bool finished = false;
   200	
   201	    #pragma omp parallel default(none) \
   202	        shared(points, clustered, unclustered_indices, clusters, thread_best, \
   203	               round_size, finished) firstprivate(N, threshold)
   204	    {
   205	        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
   206	        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
   207	
   208	        while (true) {
   209	            #pragma omp single
   210	            {
   211	                finished = finished || unclustered_indices.empty();
   212	                round_size = unclustered_indices.size();
   213	            }
   214	
   215	            if (finished) {
   216	                break;
   217	            }
   218	
   219	            ThreadBest& local_best = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread_id)];
   220	            local_best.cardinality = -1;
   221	            local_best.seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
   222	            local_best.members.clear();
   223	
   224	            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
   225	            for (size_t i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
   226	                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
   227	                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
   228	                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold, workspace);
   229	
   230	                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
   231	                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {
   232	                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
   233	                    local_best.seed = seed;
   234	                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
   235	                }
   236	            }
   237	
   238	            #pragma omp single
   239	            {
   240	                int winning_thread = -1;
   241	                int max_cardinality = -1;
   242	                int best_seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
   243	                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
   244	
   245	                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
   246	                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
   247	                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
   248	                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
   249	                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
   250	                        winning_thread = thread;
   251	                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
   252	                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
   253	                    }
   254	                }
   255	
   256	                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
   257	                    Cluster cluster;
   258	                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
   259	                    cluster.members = std::move(
   260	                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
   261	
   262	                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
   263	                        clustered[member] = 1;
   264	                    }
   265	                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
     2

+inline double squaredDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {

+// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
+// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.
+struct CandidateWorkspace {
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
+        : membership_epoch(static_cast<size_t>(point_count), 0),
+          max_squared_distances(static_cast<size_t>(point_count)) {
+        members.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(point_count));
+    }
+
+    void startCandidate(const int seed_point) {
+        ++epoch;

+            std::fill(membership_epoch.begin(), membership_epoch.end(), 0);
+            ++epoch;

+// Cache-line separation keeps independently written per-worker results from
+// contending during a clustering round.
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    std::vector<int> members;

                                   const double threshold) {

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all sequential QT rounds.  Candidate
+    // clusters for different seeds share only read-only state and therefore
+    // scale independently; a deterministic per-thread reduction selects the
+    // same lowest-index seed on cardinality ties as the original loop.
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()));
+    size_t round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+

+               round_size, finished) firstprivate(N, threshold)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+
+        while (true) {
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = finished || unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = unclustered_indices.size();
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {

-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;

-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list

-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+

+            local_best.cardinality = -1;
+            local_best.seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+            local_best.members.clear();
+
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, unclustered_indices, points, threshold, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int winning_thread = -1;
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
+                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
+                        winning_thread = thread;
+                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
+                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(
+                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&clustered](const int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
+            }
         }
     }

+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int winning_thread = -1;
+                int max_cardinality = -1;
+                int best_seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    const ThreadBest& candidate = thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(thread)];
+                    if (candidate.cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                        (candidate.cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                         candidate.seed < best_seed)) {
+                        winning_thread = thread;
+                        max_cardinality = candidate.cardinality;
+                        best_seed = candidate.seed;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winning_thread >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+                    cluster.members = std::move(
+                        thread_best[static_cast<size_t>(winning_thread)].members);
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&clustered](const int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
+            }
         }
     }

+inline double squaredDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {

+// Scratch storage is private to an OpenMP worker and reused for every seed it
+// processes.  Epoch tags avoid clearing the membership array for every seed.
+struct CandidateWorkspace {

codex
Implemented OpenMP parallelization in-place without changing the executable name or adding files.

Key changes:

- Parallel seed evaluation using a persistent OpenMP team and dynamic scheduling.
- Deterministic reductions preserve original seed and candidate tie-breaking.
- Reusable per-thread workspaces eliminate repeated allocations and diameter rescans.
- OpenMP is now required and linked by [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r2/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Core implementation is in [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp:88).

Validation passed:

- Identical membership hashes across 1–256 threads.
- Matched reference results across 14 thresholds.
- AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer passed.
- Default 1,000-point benchmark improved from about 7.0 seconds originally to 1.03 seconds with one thread and 33 ms with 64 threads.
